AT2026-10-30#
party document Public statement

Wie gewalttätig wird die "Halloween-Nacht"? Innenminister muss Sicherheit der Bürger garantieren

FPÖ General Secretary Michael Schnedlitz criticized the Interior Minister for failing to protect Austrian citizens ahead of Halloween night, demanding comprehensive security assessments after the Foreign Ministry warned diplomats about potential violent unrest from left-extremist groups and migrant gangs, and called for the minister's resignation if violence occurs.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Dieser Schritt der dafür verantwortlichen Neos-Außenministerin Beate Meinl-Reisinger lässt nur einen Schluss zu: Die österreichischen Behörden haben Angst. Sie haben Angst vor den angekündigten Demos, zu denen linksextreme Gruppierungen für die sogenannte 'Halloween-Nacht' aufgerufen haben.
This step by the responsible Neos Foreign Minister Beate Meinl-Reisinger allows only one conclusion: The Austrian authorities are afraid. They are afraid of the announced demonstrations to which left-extremist groups have called for the so-called 'Halloween night'.
Michael Schnedlitz
Gewalt durch Linksextreme und Migranten-Banden muss schon im Vorfeld rigoros unterbunden werden. Das ist die Aufgabe für einen Innenminister.
Violence by left-extremists and migrant gangs must be rigorously prevented in advance. That is the task of an Interior Minister.
Michael Schnedlitz

CH2026-10-28#
party document Public statement

Steuergeschenke für reiche Ehepaare? Nein zur Mitte-Initiative!

The Swiss Party of Labour opposes the Centre Party's initiative on individual taxation for married couples, calling it a tax gift to wealthy couples that would cost up to three billion francs annually and lead to cuts in public services.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Die Vorlage ist ein Steuergeschenk für reiche Ehepaare, welche bis zu drei Milliarden Franken kosten und zu Kürzungen im Service public führen wird.
The proposal is a tax gift for wealthy married couples, which will cost up to three billion francs and lead to cuts in public services.
Partei der Arbeit der Schweiz
Die Vorlage ist damit im Kern ein Steuergeschenk für reiche Ehepaare.
The proposal is thus fundamentally a tax gift for wealthy married couples.
Partei der Arbeit der Schweiz

DISY has been governing for the last 13 years. First the Anastasiades Government and now the Christodoulides Government roll out the red carpet for big interests, put big profits above the interest of society, dismantle the welfare state and labor relations, leave thousands of small and medium-sized businesses at the mercy of inflation, and elevate institutional corruption and entanglement with their actions and days

Συνάντηση ΓΓ ΑΚΕΛ με την Προσωπική Απεσταλμένη του ΓΓ του ΟΗΕ για το Κυπριακό

The General Secretary of AKEL met with the UN Secretary-General's Personal Envoy on Cyprus to discuss prospects for progress toward an expanded conference before António Guterres' term ends, reiterating AKEL's position that previously achieved convergences should serve as the basis for resuming substantive negotiations and proposing a roadmap respecting the negotiating acquis including the Guterres Framework.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Από πλευράς ΑΚΕΛ, επαναλάβαμε τη θέση μας ότι οι συγκλίσεις που έχουν επιτευχθεί μέχρι σήμερα πρέπει να δοθούν στις δύο πλευρές και να αξιοποιηθούν ως βάση για την επανέναρξη ουσιαστικών διαπραγματεύσεων.
From AKEL's side, we repeated our position that the convergences achieved to date must be given to the two sides and utilized as a basis for the resumption of substantive negotiations.
AKEL
Υπογραμμίζοντας, ωστόσο, ότι τα ΜΟΕ, όσο σημαντικά και αν είναι, δεν μπορούν να υποκαταστήσουν τη σημασία της επανέναρξης ουσιαστικών διαπραγματεύσεων για συνολική λύση του Κυπριακού.
Emphasizing, however, that the Confidence Building Measures, however important they may be, cannot substitute for the importance of resuming substantive negotiations for a comprehensive solution to the Cyprus problem.
AKEL

party document Public statement

Τα ντροπιαστικά φαινόμενα εργασιακής εκμετάλλευσης αναδεικνύουν σοβαρές ανεπάρκειες στους μηχανισμούς ελέγχου και προστασίας των εργαζομένων

AKEL condemns revelations of labor trafficking and inhumane working conditions in Cyprus, criticizes the government for weakening employment controls for third-country workers, and demands full investigation, strengthened inspection mechanisms, and comprehensive revision of employment strategy for third-country workers with equal protection for all workers.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Οι αποκαλύψεις για εργασιακό trafficking και απάνθρωπες συνθήκες εργασίας και διαβίωσης εργαζομένων στην Κύπρο είναι ντροπιαστικές για τον τόπο μας.
The revelations about labor trafficking and inhumane working and living conditions of workers in Cyprus are shameful for our country.
AKEL
Αντί να ενισχύει τους ελέγχους και τις ασφαλιστικές δικλίδες προστασίας, η κυβέρνηση συναίνεσε σε απαιτήσεις των εργοδοτικών οργανώσεων για χαλάρωση των προϋποθέσεων απασχόλησης εργαζομένων από τρίτες χώρες, προς εξυπηρέτηση μεγάλων συμφερόντων αντί του ελέγχου της εργοδοτικής αυθαιρεσίας.
Instead of strengthening controls and protective safety valves, the government consented to demands from employer organizations to relax the conditions for employing workers from third countries, serving large interests instead of controlling employer arbitrariness.
AKEL

Καταφέραμε την αύξηση του επιδόματος για τους φοιτητές εξωτερικού

ELAM claims credit for securing an increase in the airline ticket subsidy for students studying abroad from €300 to €500 through its chairmanship of the Parliamentary Committee on Transport, Communications and Works and continuous pressure.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Μετά από δική μας εισήγηση, στην Κοινοβουλευτική Επιτροπή Μεταφορών, Επικοινωνιών και Έργων, της οποίας προεδρεύει το Ε.ΛΑ.Μ., αλλά και έπειτα από συνεχή άσκηση πιέσεων, επιτεύχθηκε ακόμη ένα σημαντικό βήμα στήριξης των συμπολιτών μας.
Following our proposal, in the Parliamentary Committee on Transport, Communications and Works, which is chaired by ELAM, and also after continuous pressure, another significant step was achieved in supporting our fellow citizens.
National Popular Front (ELAM)

National Popular Front (ELAM)

Alice Weidel criticized planned demonstrations in 34 German cities organized by 'Generation Z regelt', arguing that protesters are demonstrating against the consequences of left-wing policies including migration, climate ideology, and energy policy, while warning of potential violence from left-extremist and Islamist groups.

Uncontrolled migration into our country and into our social systems has hopelessly overwhelmed the education and social system as well as the housing market.
Alice Weidel

AfD federal spokesperson Alice Weidel criticized the German government for weakening Germany as an investment location after Audi announced a 4 billion euro investment in a new plant in Georgia, USA, calling for those responsible for making Germany unattractive to investors to resign.

The wake-up call that the Audi CEO has sent from the USA to Germany is deafening. The federal government must finally act.

Denn Audi zeigt schonungslos, worum es in einer Marktwirtschaft geht: Unternehmen müssen Geld verdienen und dafür verlässliche Arbeitsbedingungen vorfinden. Früher war das in Deutschland gewährleistet. Doch Merkel, Scholz und jetzt das Duo Klingbeil-Merz haben alles darangesetzt, den Wirtschaftsstandort systematisch zu schwächen

Gemeinsam mit der GenZ auf die Straße!

The Left party announces support for peaceful protests planned for October 31, 2026, organized by Generation Z under the motto "Gen Z regelt" in 32 cities across Germany, opposing social cuts, pension reforms, and the eight-hour workday abolition proposed by the Merz government.

DE2026-10-29#
party document Public statement

VW rechnet sich arm, um Druck auf die Beschäftigten zu machen

Die Linke party chair Luigi Pantisano criticized Volkswagen's reported quarterly loss as an accounting trick to pressure workers before wage negotiations, arguing that VW remains financially strong with 33 billion euros in cash and supporting the IG Metall union's demand for a five percent wage increase.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
VW meldet einen Milliardenverlust. Wer genauer hinsieht, findet vor allem einen Buchungstrick. 6,4 der 7,8 Milliarden Euro Sonderbelastungen sind eine Abschreibung auf Porsche, bei der kein einziger Euro die Kasse verlässt.
VW reports a billion-euro loss. Anyone who looks more closely finds primarily an accounting trick. 6.4 of the 7.8 billion euros in special charges are a write-down on Porsche, in which not a single euro leaves the cash register.
Luigi Pantisano
Die Beschäftigten bei VW haben eine Lohnerhöhung verdient und keine Rechentricks. Die Forderung der IG Metall nach fünf Prozent ist mehr als gerechtfertigt.
VW employees deserve a wage increase and not accounting tricks. The IG Metall demand for five percent is more than justified.
Luigi Pantisano

Oxfam-Studie: Die Privatjet-Party muss enden

Die Linke party chairman Luigi Pantisano calls for a ban on private jets and mega-yachts alongside wealth taxation, responding to an Oxfam study showing private jets produce 16 million tons of CO₂ annually, and criticizes Chancellor Merz for flying private jets to Sylt.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Die Welt steuert auf 2,8° Erwärmung zu und die Superreichen fliegen im Privatjet zum Einkaufen nach Paris. Superreiche zerstören unsere Lebensgrundlagen und treiben damit immer mehr Menschen ins Elend. Wir können uns diese Superreichen nicht mehr leisten.
The world is heading towards 2.8° warming and the super-rich fly by private jet to shop in Paris. The super-rich are destroying our livelihoods and driving more and more people into misery. We can no longer afford these super-rich.
Luigi Pantisano
Die Privatjet-Party muss enden, wenn dieses Land und diese Erde für die Menschen lebenswert bleiben soll. Wir brauchen neben einer ordentlichen Besteuerung dieser riesigen Vermögen ein Verbot von Privatjets und Mega-Yachten.
The private jet party must end if this country and this earth are to remain livable for people. In addition to proper taxation of these huge fortunes, we need a ban on private jets and mega-yachts.
Luigi Pantisano

FI2026-10-26#
party document Public statement

Idän ja lännen demokratiamallit täydentävät toisiaan

Communist Party of Finland chairperson Tiina Sandberg will speak at a Socialism seminar in Beijing in early November organized by the Chinese Academy of Social Sciences, discussing democracy-building and comparing Western liberal and Chinese socialist models.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Puheenvuoroni käsittelee demokratian rakentamisen vaikeutta
My speech will address the difficulty of building democracy
Tiina Sandberg
Länsimainen liberaali demokratia lähtee yksilön muodollisesta vapaudesta, mutta vapaus jää helposti tyhjäksi, jos valta omistamisen ja talouden avulla keskitetään vain joidenkin käsiin. Kiinan sosialistinen järjestelmä lähtee vahvemmin yhteisestä toimintakyvystä ja pitkäjänteisestä suunnittelusta
Western liberal democracy starts from the individual's formal freedom, but freedom easily becomes empty if power through ownership and economy is concentrated in only some hands. China's socialist system starts more strongly from collective capacity to act and long-term planning
Tiina Sandberg

VAS Saramo: Joululahjarahojen jako lopetettava ja VTV:n selvitettävä rahojen aiempaa käyttöä

Left Alliance MEP Jussi Saramo demands the government stop distributing so-called Christmas gift funds and calls for the National Audit Office to investigate past use of these funds, criticizing allocations to religious fundamentalist organizations and proposing redirected savings to social and health sector organizations.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Mitä se kertoo hallituksen arvoista, että rahaa tutkitusti tärkeää työtä esimerkiksi vanhusten, vammaisten tai sairaiden kanssa tekeviltä järjestöiltä vain leikataan ja leikataan, mutta hallituspuolueiden lähipiirissä toimiville naispappeutta tai aborttia vastustaville toimijoille kyllä riittää jaettavaa?
What does it say about the government's values that money is just cut and cut from organizations doing demonstrably important work with, for example, the elderly, disabled or sick, but there is enough to distribute to actors in the government parties' circles who oppose women priests or abortion?
Jussi Saramo
On kohtuutonta, että sote-järjestöjen jokainen avustuseuro syynätään tarkasti, mutta joululahjarahojen käytön asianmukaisesta valvonnasta ei ole vastaavaa varmuutta
It is unreasonable that every euro of assistance to social and health organizations is scrutinized carefully, but there is no corresponding certainty about proper supervision of the use of Christmas gift funds
Jussi Saramo

FI2026-10-29#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Mai Kivelä: Suomen on tutkittava ovatko Israelin armeijassa taistelevat suomalaiset syyllistyneet sotarikoksiin

Left Alliance MP Mai Kivelä submitted a written question to the government calling for an official investigation into whether Finnish citizens serving in the Israeli army may have committed war crimes in Gaza.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Gazan tilanne on katastrofaalinen. Israelin sotatoimet Gazassa ovat täysin ylimitoitettuja ja epäinhimillisiä. Israel suorittaa koko maailman silmien edessä kansanmurhaa. Myös Suomen on omalta osaltaan selvitettävä ovatko sen kansalaiset syyllistyneet Gazassa sotarikoksiin
The situation in Gaza is catastrophic. Israel's military operations in Gaza are completely disproportionate and inhumane. Israel is committing genocide before the eyes of the entire world. Finland must also for its part investigate whether its citizens have committed war crimes in Gaza
Mai Kivelä
Israelin sotatoimet Gazassa täyttävät useiden eri YK-elinten ja asiantuntijoiden mukaan kansanmurhan tunnusmerkistön ja sen johtajista on annettu Kansainvälisen rikostuomioistuimen pidätysmääräys. Israel on miehittänyt palestiinalaisalueita vuosikymmeniä ja rakentaa kiihtyvällä tahdilla kansainvälisen oikeuden vastaisia siirtokuntia sekä kohdistaa palestiinalaisiin sortotoimia, jotka täyttävät apartheidin tunnusmerkistön. On selvää, että Suomessa on tutkittava asiaa
According to several different UN bodies and experts, Israel's military operations in Gaza meet the criteria for genocide and arrest warrants have been issued for its leaders by the International Criminal Court. Israel has occupied Palestinian territories for decades and is building illegal settlements at an accelerating pace in violation of international law and subjects Palestinians to oppressive measures that meet the criteria for apartheid. It is clear that Finland must investigate the matter
Mai Kivelä

FI2026-10-29#
party document Public statement

Johannes Yrttiaho: VM:n avaus puolustusmenojen leikkaamisesta on kannatettava

Left Alliance MP Johannes Yrttiaho endorsed the Finance Ministry's proposal to temporarily reduce defense spending targets during the next government term, arguing that uncontrolled military spending growth threatens welfare services and prolongs recession.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Tämä on tervetullut realismin pilkahdus ja keskustelunavaus valtionvarainministeriöltä
This is a welcome glimpse of realism and opening for discussion from the Finance Ministry
Johannes Yrttiaho
Sotilasmenojen hallitsematon kasvu heikentää julkista taloutta erittäin merkittävästi. Se on keskeinen valtionvelan kasvun syy, ja se on tarkoittanut jo nyt toteutettavia kovia sosiaali- ja terveyspalveluiden, sosiaaliturvan ja koulutuksen leikkauksia.
The uncontrolled growth of military spending weakens public finances very significantly. It is a central reason for the growth of state debt, and it has already meant hard cuts being implemented in social and health services, social security and education.
Johannes Yrttiaho

FI2026-10-28#
party document Parliamentary intervention

VAS Andersson: Amazonin algoritmiseen riistoon tulee puuttua uusilla EU-säännöillä

Left Alliance MEP Li Andersson visited Amazon warehouse facilities in Germany and Poland with the European Parliament's Employment Committee to investigate working conditions and algorithmic management, calling for new EU regulations on algorithmic management and artificial intelligence use in workplaces.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Osoittautui käytännössä mahdottomaksi saada kunnolla tietoa työn valvontaan ja tehokkuuden seurantaan käytettävien järjestelmien toiminnasta. Työntekijät eivät tiedä, mitä dataa kerätään ja mihin sitä käytetään. Kiristyvät tehokkuusvaatimukset kuitenkin aiheuttavat vaaratilanteita ja kuormitusta. Riisto muuttuu näkymättömäksi, kun sen suorittaa algoritmi.
It proved practically impossible to properly obtain information about the functioning of systems used for work monitoring and efficiency tracking. Workers do not know what data is collected and what it is used for. Tightening efficiency requirements, however, cause dangerous situations and strain. Exploitation becomes invisible when it is carried out by an algorithm.
Li Andersson
Amazonin Bezosin ja Teslan Muskin kaltaisille teknologiajättien johtajille on periaatekysymys, että ammattiliittojen kanssa ei neuvotella. Tässä ajattelussa esimerkiksi palkka ja työehdot kuuluvat työnantajan määräysvaltaan. Samalla kuulimme työntekijöiltä siitä, että liittoon kuuluvia tai lakkoon osallistuvia työntekijöitä painostetaan tai jopa irtisanotaan.
For tech giant leaders like Amazon's Bezos and Tesla's Musk, it is a matter of principle that they do not negotiate with trade unions. In this thinking, for example, wages and working conditions belong to the employer's control. At the same time, we heard from workers that union members or workers participating in strikes are pressured or even dismissed.
Li Andersson

Left Alliance MP Pia Lohikoski criticized the Ministry of Finance's proposals for public spending cuts, arguing they would push Finland toward a class society by drastically cutting education, research, student grants, and child benefits.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
On järkyttävää, että valtiovarainministeriön mukaan suomalaisesta koulutuksesta ja tutkimuksesta voitaisiin leikata näin rajusti.
It is shocking that according to the Ministry of Finance, Finnish education and research could be cut so drastically.
Pia Lohikoski
Suomen kaltainen pieni maa pärjää vain panostamalla koulutukseen, osaamiseen ja kaikkien lasten ja nuorten hyvinvointiin ja tasa-arvoon.
A small country like Finland can only succeed by investing in education, competence, and the well-being and equality of all children and young people.
Pia Lohikoski

Rosebank lobbying scandal – Green Party responds

Green Party leader Zack Polanski condemned revelations that oil and gas firms met UK ministers 200 times between July 2024 and June 2026 regarding Rosebank and Jackdaw projects, calling for rejection of new drilling, a tightened windfall tax, and an end to fossil fuel tax breaks.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
This summer we saw nearly 3000 people die from extreme heat while wildfires raged across the country. This is the crucial decade for climate action – yet Labour ministers are still holding cozy meetings with oil and gas bosses with alarming frequency.
Zack Polanski
Budget documents revealed the government is paving the way to greenlight Rosebank which would be catastrophic for the climate. But it's no wonder this Labour government is making decisions that put the profits of oil and gas giants above the safety of people in this country, when they are constantly rubbing shoulders with them.
Zack Polanski

Lobbyists should not be in the driving seat when it comes to data centres and AI

Green Party Deputy Leader Rachel Millward criticized the Labour government for allowing AI industry lobbyists disproportionate access while excluding environmental and community groups from data centre policy decisions, calling for a halt to new data centre approvals due to water and carbon concerns.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated

Rachel Millward

Greens urge Chancellor not to shy away from taxing extreme wealth and profits as concerns rise over business-as-usual Budget

The Green Party proposed measures to raise over £60 billion through taxing extreme wealth and profits, including aligning Capital Gains Tax with income tax, introducing a wealth tax on multimillionaires, and a windfall tax on banks, while calling on Chancellor John Healey to adopt these proposals in the upcoming Budget.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
While millions of households struggle to cope with the cost-of-living crisis, successive governments have permitted a handful of super-rich individuals and corporations to rake in ever-more extreme profits – and to pay proportionally less tax on that wealth. This cannot continue.
Ellie Chowns
Britain needs a genuinely bold Budget – one which taxes extreme wealth and profits fairly, makes life more affordable for struggling households and invests in our long-term climate resilience. Huge inequality and climate breakdown cannot be tackled by tinkering around the edges.
Ellie Chowns

Joint leadership statement on democratic engagement in the Green Party

The Green Party of England and Wales leadership announced the establishment of a working group to review and propose reforms to the party's policy development structures, with initial proposals expected at Spring Conference.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated

Green Party of England and Wales leadership
We have established a working group to look at examples from across the world of how progressive parties with mass memberships develop effective policy and put forward proposals for models that could work well for our party.
Green Party of England and Wales leadership

party document Mobilisation or protest

Ανακοίνωση ΤΕ Ανατολικής Αττικής για λιμάνι Λαυρίου

ANTARSYA's Eastern Attica branch calls for protests on 30 and 31 October against the privatisation of Lavrio port and its potential acquisition by Israeli military industry, supporting an international dockworkers' strike and demanding cancellation of the port concession and an end to Greek-Israeli military cooperation.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Η ΑΝΤΑΡΣΥΑ στηρίζει τη διεθνή απεργία των λιμενεργατών στις 30 Οκτώβρη και τους αγώνες ενάντια στη μετατροπή των λιμανιών σε κρίκους της πολεμικής μηχανής.
ANTARSYA supports the international dockworkers' strike on 30 October and the struggles against the transformation of ports into links in the war machine.
ANTARSYA
Η πιθανή απόκτηση του λιμανιού από μια τέτοια εταιρεία θα μετατρέψει το Λαύριο σε πολεμικό λιμάνι στην υπηρεσία της γενοκτονίας του παλαιστινιακού λαού.
The potential acquisition of the port by such a company will transform Lavrio into a military port in the service of the genocide of the Palestinian people.
ANTARSYA

GR2026-11-01#
party document Public statement

Η Ευρώπη του αύριο χτίζεται ερήμην των λαών της και η Ελλάδα οδεύει προς δημογραφική κατάρρευση

NIKI MP Aspasia Kouroupaki criticised EU-India labour mobility agreements and the Mitsotakis government's immigration policy, arguing they prioritise business needs over addressing Greece's demographic crisis and youth emigration.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Η κυβέρνηση Μητσοτάκη δείχνει ιδιαίτερο ενδιαφέρον για την κάλυψη των αναγκών των επιχειρήσεων, χωρίς να έχει παρουσιάσει μια εξίσου αποτελεσματική πολιτική για την επιστροφή των Ελλήνων που έφυγαν και τη συγκράτηση εκείνων που ετοιμάζονται να φύγουν.
The Mitsotakis government shows particular interest in covering the needs of businesses, without having presented an equally effective policy for the return of Greeks who left and the retention of those preparing to leave.
Aspasia Kouroupaki
Ας εξηγήσουν, λοιπόν, οι κυβερνώντες πώς οραματίζονται την Ελλάδα του 2050, πόσοι νέοι Έλληνες θα μπορούν να ζουν και να εργάζονται εδώ, ποιες οικογένειες θα στηρίζουν την ελληνική περιφέρεια και πώς σκοπεύουν να αντιμετωπίσουν τη συνεχή δημογραφική συρρίκνωση.
Let the rulers explain, then, how they envision Greece in 2050, how many young Greeks will be able to live and work here, which families will support the Greek periphery and how they intend to address the continuous demographic decline.
Aspasia Kouroupaki

GR2026-11-01#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Funds και servicers δεν μπορούν να αποφασίζουν για τη ζωή των δανειοληπτών

NIKH MP Andreas Voryllas intervened in the Economic Affairs Committee to demand withdrawal of Article 23 permitting foreclosure after three missed payments, which was subsequently withdrawn by Minister Markopoulos, and submitted a legislative proposal granting borrowers a right of first refusal before non-performing loan transfers.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Θα ήθελα να σταθούμε στην τρίτη συνεδρίαση της Επιτροπής Οικονομικών Υποθέσεων, κατά την οποία ζητήσαμε ρητά την απόσυρση του άρθρου 23, το οποίο επέτρεπε τον πλειστηριασμό, αν τρεις συνεχόμενες δόσεις της ρύθμισης παρέμεναν απλήρωτες από τον δανειολήπτη. Ο κ. Μαρκόπουλος ανακοίνωσε την απόσυρσή του, μετά τις αντιδράσεις φορέων και αντιπολίτευσης.
I would like to focus on the third session of the Economic Affairs Committee, during which we explicitly requested the withdrawal of Article 23, which permitted foreclosure if three consecutive installments of the arrangement remained unpaid by the borrower. Mr. Markopoulos announced its withdrawal, following reactions from bodies and the opposition.
Andreas Voryllas
Σε αυτή τη συζήτηση η ΝΙΚΗ εισφέρει συγκεκριμένη πρόταση νόμου για προηγούμενο δικαίωμα προτίμησης του δανειολήπτη. Πριν από τη μεταβίβαση μη εξυπηρετούμενης απαίτησης, ο πιστωτής οφείλει να γνωστοποιεί ατομικά το οικονομικό αντάλλαγμα και να προσφέρει στον οφειλέτη δυνατότητα οριστικής εξόφλησης με οικονομικά ισοδύναμους όρους.
In this discussion, NIKH contributes a specific legislative proposal for a prior right of preference for the borrower. Before the transfer of a non-performing claim, the creditor must individually disclose the financial consideration and offer the debtor the possibility of final settlement on economically equivalent terms.
Andreas Voryllas

GR2026-11-01#
party document Public statement

Τα «ήρεμα νερά» της εθνικής αυτοϋπονόμευσης

Retired Lieutenant General Georgios Nikolakos, writing for Democratic Patriotic Movement – Victory, accuses the Mitsotakis government of practicing undeclared self-limitation in Greek foreign policy toward Turkey, arguing that the pursuit of "calm waters" has become a constraint preventing Greece from exercising sovereign rights rather than a diplomatic tactic.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Μήπως τα «ήρεμα νερά» δεν αποτελούν μόνο μια τακτική αποκλιμάκωσης, αλλά την πολιτική ονομασία ενός αδήλωτου αυτοπεριορισμού;
Perhaps the 'calm waters' are not just a de-escalation tactic, but the political name for an undeclared self-limitation?
Georgios Nikolakos
Ύστερα από επτά χρόνια δικής του διακυβέρνησης, ποια τουρκική αξίωση έχει υποχωρήσει και ποια ελληνική δυνατότητα έχει ενισχυθεί;
After seven years of his governance, which Turkish claim has retreated and which Greek capability has been strengthened?
Georgios Nikolakos

GR2026-11-01#
party document Public statement

Ενδεικτικό ανωριμότητας το να ψάχνουμε το πρόσωπο που θα κερδίσει τον κ. Μητσοτάκη και να μη συζητάμε για τις πολιτικές

SYRIZA-PS President Rena Dourou criticised the focus on finding a person to defeat Prime Minister Mitsotakis rather than discussing policies, emphasised the party's statutory obligation to seek programmatic alliances with progressive forces, and warned against the rise of the far-right among Greek youth.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Είναι ενδεικτικό ανωριμότητας, όχι μόνο της Αριστεράς αλλά ολόκληρης της ελληνικής κοινωνίας, να ψάχνει το πρόσωπο που θα κερδίσει τον κ. Μητσοτάκη και να μη συζητάμε για τις πολιτικές που πρέπει να υλοποιήσουμε
It is indicative of immaturity, not only of the Left but of the entire Greek society, to search for the person who will defeat Mr. Mitsotakis and not discuss the policies we need to implement
Rena Dourou
Εμείς από το καταστατικό μας είμαστε υποχρεωμένοι να αναζητήσουμε προγραμματικές συμμαχίες και προγραμματικές συγκλίσεις με προοδευτικά κόμματα και προοδευτικούς πολίτες
We are obliged by our statute to seek programmatic alliances and programmatic convergences with progressive parties and progressive citizens
Rena Dourou

GR2026-11-01#
party document Parliamentary intervention

«Οι servicers απουσίαζαν. Αιτούμαστε νέα συνεδρίαση με μοναδικούς καλεσμένους τους εκπροσώπους τους»

SYRIZA-PS Secretary Nikos Pappas submitted an urgent request for an extraordinary session of the Parliamentary Economic Affairs Committee on 3 November 2026 to hear representatives of loan servicing companies (servicers) who were absent from a scheduled hearing on 30 October, demanding their testimony before a bill is voted on in plenary.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Οι εκπρόσωποι των εταιρειών απουσίαζαν από την ακρόαση φορέων της 30ής Οκτωβρίου, παρότι είχαν προσκληθεί.
The representatives of the companies were absent from the hearing of bodies on 30 October, although they had been invited.
SYRIZA-PS
Οι εταιρείες που διαχειρίζονται τα δάνεια και τα σπίτια εκατοντάδων χιλιάδων πολιτών οφείλουν να λογοδοτήσουν στη Βουλή. Πριν ψηφιστεί το νομοσχέδιο.
The companies that manage the loans and homes of hundreds of thousands of citizens must be held accountable to Parliament. Before the bill is voted on.
Nikos Pappas

party document Public statement

Α. Λατινοπούλου: “Μητσοτάκης: εξαφανίζει την ιδιοκατοίκηση και στρώνει τον δρόμο στα funds να πάρουν τα σπίτια των Ελλήνων!”

Voice of Reason leader Afroditi Latinopoulou criticized the Mitsotakis government for attempting to pass Article 23, which would have converted private loan settlement agreements into enforceable titles without prior court decisions, and accused the government of facilitating foreclosures by funds while home ownership declined from 75.4% in 2019 to 69.4% in 2025.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Την ώρα που η Ελλάδα βιώνει μια πρωτοφανή στεγαστική κρίση, που τα ενοίκια έχουν εκτοξευθεί και χιλιάδες ελληνικές οικογένειες αγωνιούν για το αν θα έχουν αύριο ένα κεραμίδι πάνω από το κεφάλι τους, η κυβέρνηση Μητσοτάκη επιχείρησε να περάσει μια εξοργιστική διάταξη που θα άνοιγε ακόμη περισσότερο τον δρόμο στα funds για κατασχέσεις και πλειστηριασμούς!
At a time when Greece is experiencing an unprecedented housing crisis, when rents have skyrocketed and thousands of Greek families are struggling over whether they will have a roof over their heads tomorrow, the Mitsotakis government attempted to pass an outrageous provision that would open the road even further for funds to carry out seizures and auctions!
Afroditi Latinopoulou
Το 2019, όταν ανέλαβε η Νέα Δημοκρατία, η ιδιοκατοίκηση στην Ελλάδα βρισκόταν στο 75,4%. Το 2025 κατρακύλησε στο 69,4%!
In 2019, when New Democracy took office, home ownership in Greece stood at 75.4%. In 2025 it plummeted to 69.4%!
Afroditi Latinopoulou

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Η ΦΩΝΗ ΛΟΓΙΚΗΣ απαιτεί τρία απλά βήματα για να τελειώνουμε με την εγκληματικότητα των γύφτων αλλά και κάθε κακοποιού.
Voice of Reason demands three simple steps to finish with the criminality of gypsies but also every criminal.
Voice of Reason
Three strikes: Δηλαδή συλλαμβάνεσαι πρώτη φορά για κάτι; Μικρή ποινή. Συλλαμβάνεσαι δεύτερη φορά για το ίδιο ή άλλο έγκλημα; Πολύ μεγαλύτερη ποινή. Συλλαμβάνεσαι για τρίτη φορά; Μέσα τουλάχιστον για 25 χρόνια.
Three strikes: That is, you are arrested for the first time for something? Small penalty. You are arrested a second time for the same or another crime? Much larger penalty. You are arrested for a third time? Inside for at least 25 years.
Voice of Reason

party document Parliamentary intervention

Widespread Abuse of Workers at Irish-run Data Centre Construction Sites in Europe

Paul Murphy TD requested that Tom Parlon, chairman of the Irish Data Centre Supplier Alliance, return to the Oireachtas AI committee to explain his earlier statement that Irish data centre construction companies operate within labour legislation frameworks overseas, following an Investigate Europe report documenting widespread worker abuses at European construction sites involving Irish companies.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
The Investigate Europe report published this morning shows widespread abuse of workers by data construction companies, including Irish companies, at sites across Europe.
Paul Murphy
I have now written to the Oireachtas AI committee requesting that Tom Parlon be invited back to the committee to address these revelations and to explain why he told the committee meeting that he was absolutely confident that Irish companies involved in building data centres overseas are operating within the framework of labour legislation.
Paul Murphy

IE2026-10-30#
party document Mobilisation or protest

PBP Host Meeting on Daisy Hill

People Before Profit organised a public meeting on 2nd November at Ballybot House to discuss community and workplace organising to protect maternity services at Daisy Hill Hospital from suspension, with parliamentary leader Richard Boyd Barrett headlining alongside former Unison organiser Rebekah Corbett and PBP representative Marc Mac Seáin.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
We've seen cut after cut for years at Daisy Hill. Consultants leaving, services removed, beds & wards cut. The only times this have been paused or reversed even temporarily have been in the face of large community mobilisations through the SOS Daisy Hill campaign.
Marc Mac Seáin
Our politicians most particularly those in Stormont have miserably failed to stem this tide. In fact they've given political cover to service collapse through support for centralisation through the Bengoa Report.
Marc Mac Seáin

Carroll: DUP Will Pocket the Wage and Wreck the Work

People Before Profit MLA Gerry Carroll criticised the DUP for continuing to draw salaries while obstructing Assembly business through the Petition of Concern, citing their blocking of measures including raising the age of criminal responsibility, the Good Jobs Bill, and the ban on conversion practices.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
Strip away the bluster and the DUP's message is simple: they'll keep their wages and ministerial cars, and spend what's left of this mandate obstructing anything that might materially improve people's lives.
Gerry Carroll
If a nurse, care worker or classroom assistant announced they'd spend the next six months deliberately not doing their job, they'd be sacked. Yet the DUP expects a pay packet for it.
Gerry Carroll

party document Public statement

Carroll: Court Ruling a Victory for Garvaghy Residents and People Power

People Before Profit MLA Gerry Carroll welcomed a High Court ruling dismissing the Orange Order's challenge to the ban on the Drumcree parade past Garvaghy Road, calling it a victory for residents and community organizing.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
Today's ruling is a victory for the people of the Garvaghy Road. For nearly three decades they have said no to a sectarian march being forced past their homes, and today the High Court confirmed that the decision to ban it was lawful.
Gerry Carroll
Ultimately, this was won by a community that organised, stood its ground and drew support from thousands of people across Ireland. That's the real lesson of Garvaghy: when working class people stand together to oppose the politics of reactionary hate, they win.
Gerry Carroll

People Before Profit Want to Ban Data Centres - You Do Too!

People Before Profit calls for a comprehensive ban on new data centres, citing that they consume over 23% of Ireland's electricity while paying half the residential rate, and proposes a €1.5 billion annual levy to fund household energy credits, building on parliamentary bills introduced in 2021 and 2023.

Plan to Send Irish Soldiers within EU Military Mission in Lebanon Exposes Real Purpose of Ending Triple Lock

People Before Profit TD Paul Murphy criticized the government's plan to send Irish soldiers to Lebanon as part of an EU military mission without UN mandate, arguing this exposes the real purpose of ending the Triple Lock mechanism and calling for a referendum on the issue.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
Former President Michael D Higgins' was correct when he said recently that the government is not being honest and he's also correct when he says that the removal of the Triple Lock is a preparatory move towards a military alliance.
Paul Murphy
The Government is trying to redefine neutrality to just mean not joining NATO, while at the same time it is preparing to send Irish soldiers to Lebanon as part of an EU military mission. So it's very clear that ending the Triple Lock, as Michael D said, is a preparatory move towards a military alliance.
Paul Murphy

Esercito: quattro vite spezzate mentre servivano Italia, vicini a loro cari

Prime Minister Giorgia Meloni and Defense Minister Guido Crosetto laid roses at Circo Massimo to honor four Italian soldiers who died in Bosnia, establishing a public memorial site at the Defense Village ahead of November 4th commemorations.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Simone, Claudio, Franco Adriano, Laura. Quattro vite spezzate mentre servivano l'Italia. Oggi il mio pensiero è per loro e per chi li ama.
Simone, Claudio, Franco Adriano, Laura. Four lives cut short while serving Italy. Today my thoughts are with them and those who love them.
Giorgia Meloni
Abbiamo voluto che il Villaggio della Difesa, allestito in vista del 4 novembre, diventasse un luogo di raccoglimento aperto a tutti.
We wanted the Defense Village, set up ahead of November 4th, to become a place of gathering open to everyone.
Giorgia Meloni

party document Public statement

Siher: con nuove chat si rivaluti posizione. Solidarietà a Donzelli per minacce

Brothers of Italy deputy group leaders Augusta Montaruli and Stefano Maullu called for re-evaluation of Youssef Siher's legal position based on newly emerged chat messages allegedly showing organized violent activity, and expressed solidarity with Giovanni Donzelli over reported threats.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Dalla lettura delle nuove chat di Youssef Siher emerge chiaramente la presenza di una vera e propria organizzazione che, addirittura, consigliava scorte di cibo e acqua a coloro che erano chiamati a distruggere vetrine e tirare "merda agli sbirri".
From reading the new chats of Youssef Siher, the presence of a real organization clearly emerges that even advised food and water supplies to those who were called to destroy shop windows and throw 'shit at the cops'.
Augusta Montaruli
Quanto si legge è sufficiente ad approfondire ulteriormente la condotta di Siher e di rivalutare la sua posizione e la gravità dell'accusa. Parole che adombrano l'ipotesi di terrorismo, dinanzi alle quali è giustificato superare qualsiasi scrupolo, perchè difendere l'Italia è prioritario.
What we read is sufficient to further investigate Siher's conduct and to re-evaluate his position and the seriousness of the accusation. Words that hint at the hypothesis of terrorism, in the face of which it is justified to overcome any scruple, because defending Italy is a priority.
Augusta Montaruli

party document Policy proposal

Dehors: regole chiare e tempi dimezzati per imprese

Brothers of Italy senators praise a Council of Ministers decree reorganizing outdoor seating (dehors) regulations, reducing authorization times to 45 days and limiting cultural heritage oversight to areas directly adjacent to exceptional monuments.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Con il via libera del Consiglio dei ministri al decreto di riordino sui dehors, il governo Meloni mette finalmente la parola fine alla stagione delle proroghe emergenziali e dell'incertezza normativa, consegnando a bar, ristoranti e pubblici esercizi una disciplina organica, stabile e moderna.
With the Council of Ministers' approval of the decree reorganizing dehors, the Meloni government finally puts an end to the season of emergency extensions and regulatory uncertainty, delivering to bars, restaurants and public establishments an organic, stable and modern discipline.
Salvo Pogliese
Tra le principali novità previste: la riduzione a 45 giorni dei termini per il rilascio delle autorizzazioni culturali e paesaggistiche, l'applicazione del silenzio-assenso e l'istituzione di un sistema informativo delle aree interessate.
Among the main innovations provided: the reduction to 45 days of the terms for issuing cultural and landscape authorizations, the application of tacit consent and the establishment of an information system for the areas concerned.
Silvio Giovine

IT2026-10-29#
party document Policy proposal

Polizia penitenziaria: piano assunzioni più grande di ultimi 40 anni

Undersecretary of Justice Alberto Balboni announced the largest prison police recruitment plan in 40 years, with hiring in January and February expected to bring staffing close to full capacity, and noted the government renewed the collective contract for officers after 10 years.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Questo governo ha trovato una situazione molto difficile. Dopo oltre 10 anni del blocco del turnover e un ridimensionamento delle scuole di formazione, ci siamo trovati con uno stato del personale difficilissimo da gestire, con un'età media molto elevata.
This government found a very difficult situation. After over 10 years of turnover freeze and a downsizing of training schools, we found ourselves with a very difficult staffing situation to manage, with a very high average age.
Alberto Balboni
Per questo abbiamo varato il più grande piano assunzionale degli ultimi 40 anni; ciò nonostante siamo ancora sotto organico. Con il reclutamento di gennaio e febbraio ci avvicineremo finalmente alla saturazione della pianta organica.
For this reason we launched the largest recruitment plan of the last 40 years; nevertheless we are still understaffed. With the recruitment in January and February we will finally approach full staffing capacity.
Alberto Balboni

IT2026-10-28#
party document Public statement

Cortei: scarcerazione Siher mortifica lavoro forze dell’ordine

Prime Minister Giorgia Meloni criticized a Milan judge's decision not to validate the arrest of Youssef Siher, accused of managing a Telegram page that incited protests leading to vandalism in Milan, calling it shameful and a mortification of law enforcement work.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Lo dirò senza mezzi termini: considero vergognosa la decisione del gip di Milano di non convalidare l'arresto di Youssef Siher, accusato di essere il gestore della pagina Telegram 'Movimento 17 ottobre', cioè la pagina che incitava l'organizzazione di quelle manifestazioni che poi abbiamo visto portare alla devastazione di diversi esercizi commerciali a Milano.
I will say it without mincing words: I consider shameful the decision of the Milan investigating judge not to validate the arrest of Youssef Siher, accused of being the manager of the Telegram page 'Movimento 17 ottobre', that is the page that incited the organization of those demonstrations that we then saw lead to the devastation of several commercial establishments in Milan.
Giorgia Meloni
Penso che questa sia una mortificazione insopportabile del lavoro delle forze dell'ordine, degli inquirenti, del ministero dell'Interno e, in generale, di tutti coloro che si adoperano per difendere la gente per bene dal rischio di vedere devastate le proprie città e i propri esercizi commerciali.
I think this is an unbearable mortification of the work of law enforcement, investigators, the Ministry of the Interior and, in general, of all those who work to defend decent people from the risk of seeing their cities and their commercial establishments devastated.
Giorgia Meloni

IT2026-10-28#
party document Legal action

Mafia: arrestato sindaco sinistra che fomentava protesta tir

Brothers of Italy Senator Raoul Russo and party members in the Anti-Mafia Commission commented on the arrest of Corigliano Rossano mayor Flavio Stasi on mafia-related charges, criticizing left-wing parties for their silence and noting Stasi had recently led trucker protests against the Meloni government.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Stamane è stata compiuta una vasta azione contro la 'Ndrangheta che, oltre a portare all'arresto di vari esponenti di famiglie mafiose, ha portato anche all'arresto del sindaco di Corigliano Rossano, Flavio Stasi, cui sono state mosse accuse pesantissime, fra cui reati connessi all'immigrazione. Stasi è stato fino a pochi giorni fa impegnato a fianco delle proteste degli autotrasportatori non lesinando attacchi alla premier Meloni. E oggi è stato arrestato. Da parte della sinistra non si è ancora levata nessuna voce quando, per molto meno, questa voce l'hanno fatta sentire. Questa è la dimostrazione che a sinistra hanno la coda di paglia e, come al solito, applicano due pesi e due misure.
This morning a vast action was carried out against the 'Ndrangheta which, in addition to leading to the arrest of various members of mafia families, also led to the arrest of the mayor of Corigliano Rossano, Flavio Stasi, who has been charged with very serious accusations, including crimes related to immigration. Until a few days ago Stasi was engaged alongside the truck drivers' protests, not sparing attacks on Premier Meloni. And today he was arrested. From the left not a single voice has yet been raised when, for much less, they made their voice heard. This is proof that the left has a guilty conscience and, as usual, applies double standards.
Raoul Russo
Cosa hanno da dire oggi Giuseppe Conte, Elly Schlein, Pasquale Tridico, Anna Laura Orrico e Vittoria Baldino, che hanno sostenuto con entusiasmo Stasi facendone il modello del 'campo largo' in Calabria?
What do Giuseppe Conte, Elly Schlein, Pasquale Tridico, Anna Laura Orrico and Vittoria Baldino, who enthusiastically supported Stasi making him the model of the 'broad coalition' in Calabria, have to say today?
Brothers of Italy Anti-Mafia Commission members

Giustizia: tutela vittime reato entra in Costituzione

Brothers of Italy senators announce a constitutional reform inserting crime victim protection into Article 24 of the Italian Constitution, emphasizing state duty to protect victims of violence, stalking, and families of state servants.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
La tutela delle vittime entra nella Costituzione e segna un passaggio storico per la nostra Nazione.
The protection of victims enters the Constitution and marks a historic passage for our Nation.

Per troppo tempo abbiamo definito con grande precisione le garanzie dell'imputato, lasciando invece in secondo piano chi ha subito un reato.
For too long we have defined with great precision the guarantees of the accused, leaving instead in the background those who have suffered a crime.
Andrea De Priamo

30 ANNI CON IL POPOLO, CONTRO IL POTERE

Forza Nuova announces its 5th Congress will be held in Rome on January 23-24, framing 2027 as a struggle between power and the people, with revolutionary rhetoric about sweeping away enemies.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated

On January 23 and 24, the 5th Congress of Forza Nuova will be held in Rome
Forza Nuova
Il 2027 preannuncia una lotta particolarmente aspra: da una parte il potere barricato nelle aule di una giustizia di parte e corrotta e di un parlamento vile ed incapace, e dall'altra un Popolo che alza la testa quando la sua dignità, la sua identità e libertà vengono calpestate.
2027 heralds a particularly harsh struggle: on one side power barricaded in the halls of a partisan and corrupt justice system and a cowardly and incapable parliament, and on the other a People who raise their heads when their dignity, identity and freedom are trampled.

The Italian Communist Party's Umbria regional branch will inaugurate its new regional headquarters in Perugia on October 31, 2026, with national secretary Mauro Alboresi attending, positioning the space as open to citizens, political forces, unions, and social movements for discussion and collective action.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
L'apertura della nuova sede vuole rappresentare qualcosa di più di un semplice momento organizzativo: uno spazio fisico aperto alla discussione, al confronto, alla partecipazione e alla costruzione di iniziative comuni.
The opening of the new headquarters aims to represent something more than a simple organizational moment: a physical space open to discussion, debate, participation and the construction of common initiatives.
PCI Umbria

The PCI considers the unity of the left a necessary condition to build an alternative political and social bloc, capable of connecting experiences, movements, organizations and different subjects around common objectives.

The Italian Communist Party condemned the Italian government's abstention at the UN General Assembly vote on ending the US embargo against Cuba, participated in solidarity protests on 27 October 2026, and accused the Meloni government of servility to US policy and authoritarian fascist tendencies.

EX ILVA: nazionalizzare per salvare la siderurgia pubblica e difendere il lavoro

The Italian Communist Party demands nationalization of ex-ILVA steel plants to prevent closures and job losses, criticizing privatization policies and proposing public management to guarantee employment, environmental remediation, and industrial continuity across all facilities including Taranto and Genoa.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated

without the nationalization of ex-ILVA, the only serious solution to save a strategic asset of Italian industry, we are heading toward the closure of the plants and the dispersal of an industrial patrimony fundamental for the country

It is the neoliberal logic of private profit and public losses, to which the Government continues to be subordinate
Partito Comunista Italiano

La battaglia del tetra

Potere al Popolo! celebrates Lula's fourth presidential election victory in Brazil as a model for left organizing, emphasizing the importance of grassroots mobilization, digital strategy, and maintaining alliances with social movements rather than centrist compromise.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
quando la sinistra si organizza, quando smette di parlare a sé stessa e impara a stare tra le persone, quando trasforma l'indignazione in partecipazione e la partecipazione in potere, può vincere
when the left organizes, when it stops talking to itself and learns to be among the people, when it transforms indignation into participation and participation into power, it can win
Potere al Popolo!
un riferimento perché dimostra che costruire un blocco sociale ampio, radicato nei territori, capace di unire lavoratori, giovani, movimenti femministi e comunità marginalizzate è possibile e vincente; un monito perché la vittoria elettorale è solo l'inizio, e cedere al centro è la strada più rapida verso la perdita di tutto questo capitale politico e sociale
a reference because it demonstrates that building a broad social bloc, rooted in territories, capable of uniting workers, youth, feminist movements and marginalized communities is possible and winning; a warning because electoral victory is only the beginning, and yielding to the center is the fastest road to losing all this political and social capital

party document Policy proposal

Partido Comunista Português

The Portuguese Communist Party presented its first set of amendment proposals to the 2027 State Budget, including a 15% salary increase (minimum €150) for public administration workers, a 5% pension increase (minimum €80), price regulation for food and fuel, setting bottled gas at €20, housing investment increase of €900 million, rent regulation, mortgage spread reductions, a 35% tax on real estate funds, and immediate application of maximum IMI rates on vacant properties.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Avançamos com a proposta de aumento significativo dos salários na AP, instando o Governo à negociação (como a lei determina) com os sindicatos, no sentido de garantir a recuperação do poder de compra perdido
We are advancing with the proposal for a significant increase in public administration salaries, urging the Government to negotiate (as the law requires) with the unions, in order to guarantee the recovery of lost purchasing power
Paula Santos
O valor de referência que adiantamos envolve a consideração de um aumento de 15% com um mínimo de 150€ nos salários
The reference value we are putting forward involves consideration of a 15% increase with a minimum of €150 in salaries
Paula Santos

The Portuguese Communist Party repudiates a US resolution proposal at the UN Security Council regarding Western Sahara, demanding that the Portuguese government defend the Sahrawi people's right to self-determination and oppose Morocco's autonomy plan as the sole negotiation basis.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated

The PCP considers that the Portuguese Government, in the international bodies where it is represented, namely at the UN, must assume an unequivocal public position in defense of a UN Security Council resolution: that safeguards and advocates for the fulfillment of the right to self-determination of the Sahrawi people

party document Public statement

Partido Comunista Português

The Portuguese Communist Party issued a statement demanding an end to the US blockade against Cuba, calling on the Portuguese government to support a UN resolution against the blockade and to develop cooperation with Cuba, while condemning the Trump Administration's policies and supporting the "Por Cuba! Fim ao Bloqueio!" solidarity campaign.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Cuba enfrenta há mais de 60 anos um criminoso bloqueio económico, comercial e financeiro imposto pelos EUA.
Cuba has faced for more than 60 years a criminal economic, commercial and financial blockade imposed by the USA.
Partido Comunista Português
O PCP exige o fim do bloqueio dos EUA contra Cuba, a retirada de Cuba da ilegítima e arbitrária lista dos EUA de denominados "Estados patrocinadores do terrorismo", o fim da política genocida e das ameaças da Administração Trump contra o povo cubano.
